.
Finally, define a valuation
V_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}}
of the proposition letters by

V(p_{i})=\tilde{p_{i}}
, and define

\mathcal{M}_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}}:=(W_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}},\mathbin{\subset\kern-7.4pt<},N_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}},V_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}}).

3.38 Lemma
.

The structure
\mathcal{M}_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}}
is an intuitionistic neighbourhood model.

Proof.

It is easy to see that
(W_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}},\mathbin{\subset\kern-7.4pt<})
is an intuitionistic Kripke frame.
By construction, the domain of each of the partial functions in
N_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}}
is
upward closed, so the
N_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}}
consists of intuitionistic neighbourhoods.
Besides, it follows immediately from the definition of
V_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}}
that it
maps proposition letters to upsets of
(W_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}},\mathbin{\subset\kern-7.4pt<})
.
So
\mathcal{M}_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}}
is an intuitionistic neighbourhood model.
∎

We now show that the truth lemma goes through without problems.

3.39 Lemma

(Truth lemma)
.

For all
(\Gamma,n)\in W_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}}
and
\varphi\in\mathbf{L}_{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.5 -2.5 M -2.5 -2.46 L -2.5 2.46 C -2.5 2.48 -2.48 2.5 -2.46 2.5 L 2.46 2.5 C 2.48 2.5 2.5 2.48 2.5 2.46 L 2.5 -2.46 C 2.5 -2.48 2.48 -2.5 2.46 -2.5 L -2.46 -2.5 C -2.48 -2.5 -2.5 -2.48 -2.5 -2.46 Z M 2.5 2.5}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt\!\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -2.95 M -0.03 -2.92 L -2.92 -0.03 C -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 0.01 -2.92 0.03 L -0.03 2.92 C -0.01 2.94 0.01 2.94 0.03 2.92 L 2.92 0.03 C 2.94 0.01 2.94 -0.01 2.92 -0.03 L 0.03 -2.92 C 0.01 -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 -0.03 -2.92 Z M 0 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}
we have

\varphi\in\Gamma\quad\text{iff}\quad\mathcal{M}_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}},(\Gamma,n)\Vdash\varphi.

Proof.

We prove the lemma by induction on the structure of
\varphi
.
If
\varphi=\bot
or
\varphi=p_{i}\in\Prop
then the lemma is obvious.
The induction steps for
\varphi=\psi\wedge\chi
and
\varphi=\psi\vee\chi

follow from the fact that they are interpreted locally.
The induction step for implication also proceeds as usual.

Induction step for
\varphi=\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

.

Suppose
\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

. Then
\Gamma\in\dom(a_{\psi})
,
and by induction we have that
(\Delta,m)\in\tilde{\psi}

implies
(\Delta,m)\Vdash\psi
.
Since
a_{\psi}(\Gamma^{\prime},k)=\tilde{\psi}
whenever
(\Gamma,n)\mathbin{\subset\kern-7.4pt<}(\Gamma^{\prime},k)
,
we find that
(\Gamma,n)\Vdash\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

.

.
Further, then we have

,

, which in turn

.
Therefore, by monotonicity
\vdash\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -3.57 -3.57 M -3.57 -3.51 L -3.57 3.51 C -3.57 3.55 -3.55 3.57 -3.51 3.57 L 3.51 3.57 C 3.55 3.57 3.57 3.55 3.57 3.51 L 3.57 -3.51 C 3.57 -3.55 3.55 -3.57 3.51 -3.57 L -3.51 -3.57 C -3.55 -3.57 -3.57 -3.55 -3.57 -3.51 Z M 3.57 3.57}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

hence

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -3.57 -3.57 M -3.57 -3.51 L -3.57 3.51 C -3.57 3.55 -3.55 3.57 -3.51 3.57 L 3.51 3.57 C 3.55 3.57 3.57 3.55 3.57 3.51 L 3.57 -3.51 C 3.57 -3.55 3.55 -3.57 3.51 -3.57 L -3.51 -3.57 C -3.55 -3.57 -3.57 -3.55 -3.57 -3.51 Z M 3.57 3.57}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

.
If

(because

and every element of our frame has a successor).
Also by definition this means

(\Delta,m)\mathbin{\subset\kern-7.4pt<}(\Gamma,n)

so that
\top\to\psi

\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

Induction step for

) and (

) implies

,
hence by deductive closure of theories

.

but not
\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to6.52pt{\vbox to6.52pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\;\lower-3.25974pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

, which gives an element

.

3.40 Theorem
.

We have
\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}}\varphi
if and only if

\Gamma\Vdash^{\mathrm{inm}}\varphi
if and only if
\Gamma\models\varphi
if and only if
\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{IM}}\varphi
.

Proof.

Theorem
3.34
states that
\Gamma\Vdash^{\mathrm{inm}}\varphi

if and only if
\Gamma\models\varphi
, and by Theorem
2.8

we have
\Gamma\models\varphi
if and only if
\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{IM}}\varphi
,
so we only have to prove the first “iff.”
If
\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}}\varphi
, then Theorem
3.9

gives
\Gamma\Vdash^{\mathrm{inm}}\varphi
. We prove the converse by contraposition.
Suppose
\Gamma\not\vdash_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}}\varphi
.
Then we can use Lemma
3.36
to find a prime theory

\Delta
extending
\Gamma
that does not contain
\varphi
.
Then we have
\mathcal{M}_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}},(\Delta,0)\not\Vdash\Gamma

and
\mathcal{M}_{\mathsf{IM_{Calc}}},(\Delta,0)\Vdash\varphi

by Lemma
3.39
, so that
\Gamma\not\Vdash^{\mathrm{inm}}\varphi
.
∎

4  Relation to other logics

In order to position
\mathsf{IM}
into the landscape of existing intuitionistic
modal logics, we investigate the relation of
\mathsf{IM}
to various other
intuitionistic monotone modal logics, as well as to its normal
modal counterpart
\mathsf{IK}
.

4.1  Comparing intuitionistic monotone modal logics

There are two intuitionistic counterparts of monotone modal logic that often
appear in the literature: constructive monotone modal logic

\mathsf{WM}

[
13
,
12
]
,
and the extension of intuitionistic logic with a single monotone
modality
\triangledown
, which we denote by
\mathsf{iM}
.
We write
\mathbf{L}_{\triangledown}
for the language over which
\mathsf{iM}
is defined.
A generalised Hilbert calculus for
\mathsf{iM}
can be obtained by reading
\triangledown
as

and deleting (
\mathsf{Mon_{\hskip 0.62221pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -2.95 M -0.03 -2.92 L -2.92 -0.03 C -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 0.01 -2.92 0.03 L -0.03 2.92 C -0.01 2.94 0.01 2.94 0.03 2.92 L 2.92 0.03 C 2.94 0.01 2.94 -0.01 2.92 -0.03 L 0.03 -2.92 C 0.01 -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 -0.03 -2.92 Z M 0 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.62221pt}}
) in Definition
2.11
.

In this section we consider the connection between
\mathsf{iM}
,
\mathsf{WM}
and
\mathsf{IM}
.
First, we recall
constructive neighbourhood semantics
for
\mathsf{WM}

and use this to show that
\mathsf{WM}
proves strictly fewer consecutions than
\mathsf{IM}
.
We then show that
\mathsf{IM}
is sound and complete with respect to the class
of so-called
full
constructive neighbourhood models,
thereby providing yet another frame semantics for
\mathsf{IM}
.

Next, we observe that there are two obvious ways to map the language of

\mathsf{iM}
to
\mathbf{L}_{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt\!\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

, namely by viewing
\triangledown
either as
or as
.
This gives rise to a comparison of
\mathsf{iM}
with the box-free and
diamond-free fragments of
\mathsf{WM}
and
\mathsf{IM}
.
This is analogous to the study of

intuitionistic normal modal logics, see
[
28

and
[

,

]
.

4.1 Definition
.

A
constructive neighbourhood model
is a tuple

\mathcal{M}=(W,\preccurlyeq,\gamma,V)

consisting of a preordered set
(W,\preccurlyeq)
, a neighbourhood function

\gamma:W\to\mathcal{P}\mathcal{P}(W)
, and a valuation
V:\Prop\to\up(W,\preccurlyeq)
.
The interpretation of an
\mathbf{L}_{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.5 -2.5 M -2.5 -2.46 L -2.5 2.46 C -2.5 2.48 -2.48 2.5 -2.46 2.5 L 2.46 2.5 C 2.48 2.5 2.5 2.48 2.5 2.46 L 2.5 -2.46 C 2.5 -2.48 2.48 -2.5 2.46 -2.5 L -2.46 -2.5 C -2.48 -2.5 -2.5 -2.48 -2.5 -2.46 Z M 2.5 2.5}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt\!\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -2.95 M -0.03 -2.92 L -2.92 -0.03 C -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 0.01 -2.92 0.03 L -0.03 2.92 C -0.01 2.94 0.01 2.94 0.03 2.92 L 2.92 0.03 C 2.94 0.01 2.94 -0.01 2.92 -0.03 L 0.03 -2.92 C 0.01 -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 -0.03 -2.92 Z M 0 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

-formula at a world
w\in W

is defined via the usual propositional clauses and the following modal clauses:

\displaystyle\mathcal{M},w\Vdash\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -3.57 -3.57 M -3.57 -3.51 L -3.57 3.51 C -3.57 3.55 -3.55 3.57 -3.51 3.57 L 3.51 3.57 C 3.55 3.57 3.57 3.55 3.57 3.51 L 3.57 -3.51 C 3.57 -3.55 3.55 -3.57 3.51 -3.57 L -3.51 -3.57 C -3.55 -3.57 -3.57 -3.55 -3.57 -3.51 Z M 3.57 3.57}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

\displaystyle\quad\text{iff}\quad\text{for all }w^{\prime}\geq w\text{ there exists }a\in\gamma(w^{\prime})\text{ such that for all }v\in a,\text{ we have }\mathcal{M},v\Vdash\varphi

\displaystyle\mathcal{M},w\Vdash\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to6.52pt{\vbox to6.52pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\;\lower-3.25974pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -4.21 M -0.04 -4.17 L -4.17 -0.04 C -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 0.02 -4.17 0.04 L -0.04 4.17 C -0.02 4.19 0.02 4.19 0.04 4.17 L 4.17 0.04 C 4.19 0.02 4.19 -0.02 4.17 -0.04 L 0.04 -4.17 C 0.02 -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 -0.04 -4.17 Z M 0 4.21}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\varphi

\displaystyle\quad\text{iff}\quad\text{for all }w^{\prime}\geq w\text{ and all }a\in\gamma(w^{\prime})\text{ there exists }v\in a,\text{ such that }\mathcal{M},v\Vdash\varphi

It was proven in
[
12
, Section 4]
that
\mathsf{WM}
is sound and complete
with respect to the class of constructive neighbourhood models,
and the proof in fact entails strong completeness.
The same class of models can be used to interpret the language
\mathbf{L}_{\triangledown}
,
interpreting
\triangledown
in the same way as
. This clearly provides a
sound semantics, and a straightforward canonical model construction proves
(strong) completeness as well. So we have:

4.2 Theorem
.

Both
\mathsf{WM}
and
\mathsf{iM}
are sound and strongly complete with respect to the class
of constructive neighbourhood models.

The next example shows that
(\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

}

is
not derivable in
\mathsf{WM}
.
Since this is an axiom of
\mathsf{IM}
, it shows that
\mathsf{WM}
proves fewer formulas
than
\mathsf{IM}
.

4.3 Example
.

Let
W=\{w,v\}
ordered by
w\preccurlyeq w\preccurlyeq v\preccurlyeq v
.
Define
\gamma:W\to\mathcal{PP}W
by
\gamma(w)=\{\{w\}\}
and

\gamma(v)=\emptyset
.
Define a valuation
V
of the proposition letter
P
by
V(p)=\{v\}
.
Then
\mathcal{M}=(W,\preccurlyeq,\gamma,V)
is a constructive neighbourhood model.

Both
w
and
v
do not satisfy
\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -3.57 -3.57 M -3.57 -3.51 L -3.57 3.51 C -3.57 3.55 -3.55 3.57 -3.51 3.57 L 3.51 3.57 C 3.55 3.57 3.57 3.55 3.57 3.51 L 3.57 -3.51 C 3.57 -3.55 3.55 -3.57 3.51 -3.57 L -3.51 -3.57 C -3.55 -3.57 -3.57 -3.55 -3.57 -3.51 Z M 3.57 3.57}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\top
, because
\gamma(v)=\emptyset
.
Therefore

}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\top\to\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to6.52pt{\vbox to6.52pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\;\lower-3.25974pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

. But

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -4.21 M -0.04 -4.17 L -4.17 -0.04 C -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 0.02 -4.17 0.04 L -0.04 4.17 C -0.02 4.19 0.02 4.19 0.04 4.17 L 4.17 0.04 C 4.19 0.02 4.19 -0.02 4.17 -0.04 L 0.04 -4.17 C 0.02 -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 -0.04 -4.17 Z M 0 4.21}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108ptp
.

4.4 Remark
.

Various other semantics were used for
\mathsf{iM}
.
For example, in
[
13
, Section 6]
the extension of intuitionistic
logic with a monotone (box) modality was interpreted in
constructive neighbourhood models
(W,\preccurlyeq,\gamma,V)

such that (i)
\preccurlyeq
is a partial order, (ii)
\gamma(w)
is upwards closed under
inclusion, and (iii)
w\preccurlyeq w^{\prime}
implies
\gamma(w)\subseteq\gamma(w^{\prime})
.
The interpretation of the modality can then be simplified to

w\Vdash\triangledown\varphi
iff
\llbracket\varphi\rrbracket\in\gamma(w)
.

If moreover we allow only upsets as neighbourhoods, i.e. we view
\gamma
as a
map from
W
to
\mathcal{P}(\up(W,\preccurlyeq))
, then we obtain the semantics
for
\mathsf{iM}
used by Goldblatt
[
25
, Section 4]
,
and later in
[
31
, Section 8]
and
[
30
, Sections 4.1 and 4.2]
.

Since
\mathsf{WM}
is weaker than
\mathsf{IM}
, we can try to use (certain)
constructive neighbourhood models as semantics for
\mathsf{IM}
.
To this end, first we note that a constructive neighbourhood frame

(W,\preccurlyeq,\gamma,V)
satisfies
(\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -3.57 -3.57 M -3.57 -3.51 L -3.57 3.51 C -3.57 3.55 -3.55 3.57 -3.51 3.57 L 3.51 3.57 C 3.55 3.57 3.57 3.55 3.57 3.51 L 3.57 -3.51 C 3.57 -3.55 3.55 -3.57 3.51 -3.57 L -3.51 -3.57 C -3.55 -3.57 -3.57 -3.55 -3.57 -3.51 Z M 3.57 3.57}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\top\to\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to6.52pt{\vbox to6.52pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\;\lower-3.25974pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -4.21 M -0.04 -4.17 L -4.17 -0.04 C -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 0.02 -4.17 0.04 L -0.04 4.17 C -0.02 4.19 0.02 4.19 0.04 4.17 L 4.17 0.04 C 4.19 0.02 4.19 -0.02 4.17 -0.04 L 0.04 -4.17 C 0.02 -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 -0.04 -4.17 Z M 0 4.21}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\varphi)\to\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to6.52pt{\vbox to6.52pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\;\lower-3.25974pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -4.21 M -0.04 -4.17 L -4.17 -0.04 C -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 0.02 -4.17 0.04 L -0.04 4.17 C -0.02 4.19 0.02 4.19 0.04 4.17 L 4.17 0.04 C 4.19 0.02 4.19 -0.02 4.17 -0.04 L 0.04 -4.17 C 0.02 -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 -0.04 -4.17 Z M 0 4.21}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\varphi
if it
satisfies: if
\gamma(w)\neq\emptyset
and
w\leq w^{\prime}
then
\gamma(w^{\prime})\neq\emptyset
.
Let us call such constructive neighbourhood models
full
.
In passing, we note:

4.5 Proposition
.

\mathsf{iM}
is sound and strongly complete with respect to
the class of full constructive neighbourhood models.

Proof.

Soundness follows from Theorem
4.2
.
For completeness, suppose
\Gamma\not\vdash_{\mathsf{iM}}\varphi
.
Then by Theorem
4.2
there exists a constructive
neighbourhood model
(W,\preccurlyeq,\gamma,V)
falsifying the consecution.
Defining
\gamma^{\prime}
by

\gamma^{\prime}(w)=\begin{cases}\gamma(w)&\text{if }\gamma(v)\neq\emptyset\text{ for all }v\in W\text{ such that }w\leq v\\
\emptyset&\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

yields a full constructive neighbourhood model
(W,\preccurlyeq,\gamma^{\prime},V)

that leaves the interpretation of
\mathbf{L}_{\triangledown}
-formulas unchanged,
hence gives a full model falsifying the consecution.
∎

We can transform intuitionistic neighbourhood models into full
constructive neighbourhood models as follows.

4.6 Definition
.

Let
\mathcal{M}=(W,\leq,N,V)
be an intuitionistic neighbourhood frame.
For each
w\in W
, let
N_{w}=\{a\in N\mid w\in\dom(a)\}

and
{\uparrow}w=\{v\in W\mid w\leq v\}
, and define

{C}(w):=\big\{\{a(f(a))\mid a\in N_{w}\}\mid f:N_{w}\to{\uparrow}w\text{ is a function}\big\}.

Let
\widehat{W}=\{(w,\Sigma)\mid\Sigma\in{C}(w)\}

and define
(w,\Sigma)\preccurlyeq(w^{\prime},\Sigma^{\prime})
iff
w\leq w^{\prime}
.
Then
(\widehat{W},\preccurlyeq)
is a pre-ordered set.
Define the neighbourhood function
\gamma
by
\gamma(w,\Sigma):=\Sigma
,
and the valuation
\widehat{V}
by
\widehat{V}(p_{i})=\{(w,\Sigma)\in\widehat{W}\mid w\in V(p)\}
.
Then

\widehat{\mathcal{M}}:=(\widehat{W},\preccurlyeq,\gamma,\widehat{V})

is a constructive neighbourhood model.

By construction,
\widehat{\mathcal{M}}
is a full constructive neighbourhood
model. Moreover, we have:

4.7 Lemma
.

Let
\mathcal{M}=(W,\leq,N,V)
be an intuitionistic neighbourhood model
and
\widehat{\mathcal{M}}=(\widehat{W},\preccurlyeq,\gamma,\widehat{V})
its
corresponding constructive neighbourhood model.
Then for all
(w,\Sigma)\in\widehat{W}
and
\varphi\in\mathbf{L}_{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.5 -2.5 M -2.5 -2.46 L -2.5 2.46 C -2.5 2.48 -2.48 2.5 -2.46 2.5 L 2.46 2.5 C 2.48 2.5 2.5 2.48 2.5 2.46 L 2.5 -2.46 C 2.5 -2.48 2.48 -2.5 2.46 -2.5 L -2.46 -2.5 C -2.48 -2.5 -2.5 -2.48 -2.5 -2.46 Z M 2.5 2.5}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt\!\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -2.95 M -0.03 -2.92 L -2.92 -0.03 C -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 0.01 -2.92 0.03 L -0.03 2.92 C -0.01 2.94 0.01 2.94 0.03 2.92 L 2.92 0.03 C 2.94 0.01 2.94 -0.01 2.92 -0.03 L 0.03 -2.92 C 0.01 -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 -0.03 -2.92 Z M 0 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}
,

\widehat{\mathcal{M}},(w,\Sigma)\Vdash\varphi\quad\text{iff}\quad\mathcal{M},w\Vdash\varphi.

Proof.

We use induction on the structure of
\varphi
.
The cases
\varphi=\bot
and
\varphi=p_{i}\in\Prop
hold by definition.
The inductive cases for intuitionistic connectives are routine.

Inductive step for
\varphi=\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -3.57 -3.57 M -3.57 -3.51 L -3.57 3.51 C -3.57 3.55 -3.55 3.57 -3.51 3.57 L 3.51 3.57 C 3.55 3.57 3.57 3.55 3.57 3.51 L 3.57 -3.51 C 3.57 -3.55 3.55 -3.57 3.51 -3.57 L -3.51 -3.57 C -3.55 -3.57 -3.57 -3.55 -3.57 -3.51 Z M 3.57 3.57}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\psi
.

If
\mathcal{M},w\Vdash\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -3.57 -3.57 M -3.57 -3.51 L -3.57 3.51 C -3.57 3.55 -3.55 3.57 -3.51 3.57 L 3.51 3.57 C 3.55 3.57 3.57 3.55 3.57 3.51 L 3.57 -3.51 C 3.57 -3.55 3.55 -3.57 3.51 -3.57 L -3.51 -3.57 C -3.55 -3.57 -3.57 -3.55 -3.57 -3.51 Z M 3.57 3.57}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\psi
then there exists
a\in N
such that

a(w^{\prime})\subseteq\llbracket\psi\rrbracket^{\mathcal{M}}
for all
w^{\prime}\geq w
.
For each
(w^{\prime},\Sigma^{\prime})\in\widehat{W}
such that

(w,\Sigma)\preccurlyeq(w^{\prime},\Sigma^{\prime})
, we have
w\leq w^{\prime}

hence
a\in N_{w^{\prime}}
, so that
a(v)\in\Sigma^{\prime}=:\gamma(w^{\prime},\Sigma^{\prime})
for some

v\geq w^{\prime}
. Then
w\leq v
, so by induction

a(v)\subseteq\llbracket\psi\rrbracket^{\widehat{\mathcal{M}}}
.
This proves
\widehat{\mathcal{M}},(w,\Sigma)\Vdash\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -3.57 -3.57 M -3.57 -3.51 L -3.57 3.51 C -3.57 3.55 -3.55 3.57 -3.51 3.57 L 3.51 3.57 C 3.55 3.57 3.57 3.55 3.57 3.51 L 3.57 -3.51 C 3.57 -3.55 3.55 -3.57 3.51 -3.57 L -3.51 -3.57 C -3.55 -3.57 -3.57 -3.55 -3.57 -3.51 Z M 3.57 3.57}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\psi
.

Conversely, if
\mathcal{M},w\not\Vdash\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -3.57 -3.57 M -3.57 -3.51 L -3.57 3.51 C -3.57 3.55 -3.55 3.57 -3.51 3.57 L 3.51 3.57 C 3.55 3.57 3.57 3.55 3.57 3.51 L 3.57 -3.51 C 3.57 -3.55 3.55 -3.57 3.51 -3.57 L -3.51 -3.57 C -3.55 -3.57 -3.57 -3.55 -3.57 -3.51 Z M 3.57 3.57}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\psi
then for each

a\in N_{w}
there exists some
v_{a}\geq w
such that

a(v_{a})\not\subseteq\llbracket\psi\rrbracket^{\mathcal{M}}
.
But then
\Delta:=\{a(v_{a})\mid a\in N_{w}\}\in{C}(w)
,
so
(w,\Sigma)\preccurlyeq(w,\Delta)
and (by induction) there is no neighbourhood in

\gamma(w,\Delta):=\Delta
that is contained in
\llbracket\psi\rrbracket^{\widehat{\mathcal{M}}}
.
Therefore
\widehat{\mathcal{M}},(w,\Sigma)\not\Vdash\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -3.57 -3.57 M -3.57 -3.51 L -3.57 3.51 C -3.57 3.55 -3.55 3.57 -3.51 3.57 L 3.51 3.57 C 3.55 3.57 3.57 3.55 3.57 3.51 L 3.57 -3.51 C 3.57 -3.55 3.55 -3.57 3.51 -3.57 L -3.51 -3.57 C -3.55 -3.57 -3.57 -3.55 -3.57 -3.51 Z M 3.57 3.57}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\psi
.

Inductive step for
\varphi=\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to6.52pt{\vbox to6.52pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\;\lower-3.25974pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -4.21 M -0.04 -4.17 L -4.17 -0.04 C -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 0.02 -4.17 0.04 L -0.04 4.17 C -0.02 4.19 0.02 4.19 0.04 4.17 L 4.17 0.04 C 4.19 0.02 4.19 -0.02 4.17 -0.04 L 0.04 -4.17 C 0.02 -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 -0.04 -4.17 Z M 0 4.21}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\psi
.

This follows immediately from the construction.
∎

4.8 Theorem
.

The logic
\mathsf{IM}
is sound and strongly complete with respect to the class
of full constructive neighbourhood models.

Proof.

For soundness, we only have to verify that full constructive neighbourhood
models satisfy (
\mathsf{I_{\hskip 0.62221pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -2.95 M -0.03 -2.92 L -2.92 -0.03 C -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 0.01 -2.92 0.03 L -0.03 2.92 C -0.01 2.94 0.01 2.94 0.03 2.92 L 2.92 0.03 C 2.94 0.01 2.94 -0.01 2.92 -0.03 L 0.03 -2.92 C 0.01 -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 -0.03 -2.92 Z M 0 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}

). To this end, let

\mathcal{M}=(W,\preccurlyeq,\gamma,V)
be a constructive neighbourhood model
and suppose
w\in W
satisfies
\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\top\to\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to6.52pt{\vbox to6.52pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\;\lower-3.25974pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

.
To see that
w\Vdash\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to6.52pt{\vbox to6.52pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\;\lower-3.25974pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

, let

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -3.57 -3.57 M -3.57 -3.51 L -3.57 3.51 C -3.57 3.55 -3.55 3.57 -3.51 3.57 L 3.51 3.57 C 3.55 3.57 3.57 3.55 3.57 3.51 L 3.57 -3.51 C 3.57 -3.55 3.55 -3.57 3.51 -3.57 L -3.51 -3.57 C -3.55 -3.57 -3.57 -3.55 -3.57 -3.51 Z M 3.57 3.57}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

.
Since

, so by definition there must be some

For completeness, suppose
\Gamma\not\vdash_{\mathsf{IM}}\varphi
.
Then by Theorem
3.40
there exists an intuitionistic
neighbourhood model falsifying the consecution, so that
Definition
4.6
and Lemma
4.7

yield a full constructive neighbourhood model falsifying it.
∎

Therefore the following theorem can be read as saying that the logics

\mathsf{iM}
,
\mathsf{WM}
and
\mathsf{IM}
prove precisely the same diamond-free consecutions.

4.9 Theorem
.

Let
\Gamma\cup\{\varphi\}\subseteq\mathbf{L}_{\triangledown}
be a collection
of formulas. Then

\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{iM}}\varphi\quad\text{iff}\quad\Gamma_{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.5 -2.5 M -2.5 -2.46 L -2.5 2.46 C -2.5 2.48 -2.48 2.5 -2.46 2.5 L 2.46 2.5 C 2.48 2.5 2.5 2.48 2.5 2.46 L 2.5 -2.46 C 2.5 -2.48 2.48 -2.5 2.46 -2.5 L -2.46 -2.5 C -2.48 -2.5 -2.5 -2.48 -2.5 -2.46 Z M 2.5 2.5}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.5 -2.5 M -2.5 -2.46 L -2.5 2.46 C -2.5 2.48 -2.48 2.5 -2.46 2.5 L 2.46 2.5 C 2.48 2.5 2.5 2.48 2.5 2.46 L 2.5 -2.46 C 2.5 -2.48 2.48 -2.5 2.46 -2.5 L -2.46 -2.5 C -2.48 -2.5 -2.5 -2.48 -2.5 -2.46 Z M 2.5 2.5}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}\quad\text{iff}\quad\Gamma_{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.5 -2.5 M -2.5 -2.46 L -2.5 2.46 C -2.5 2.48 -2.48 2.5 -2.46 2.5 L 2.46 2.5 C 2.48 2.5 2.5 2.48 2.5 2.46 L 2.5 -2.46 C 2.5 -2.48 2.48 -2.5 2.46 -2.5 L -2.46 -2.5 C -2.48 -2.5 -2.5 -2.48 -2.5 -2.46 Z M 2.5 2.5}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces

\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}.

Proof.

Any proof
\delta
in the generalised Hilbert calculus for
\mathsf{iM}

for
\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{iM}}\varphi
gives rise to a proof for

\Gamma^{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.5 -2.5 M -2.5 -2.46 L -2.5 2.46 C -2.5 2.48 -2.48 2.5 -2.46 2.5 L 2.46 2.5 C 2.48 2.5 2.5 2.48 2.5 2.46 L 2.5 -2.46 C 2.5 -2.48 2.48 -2.5 2.46 -2.5 L -2.46 -2.5 C -2.48 -2.5 -2.5 -2.48 -2.5 -2.46 Z M 2.5 2.5}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}\vdash_{\mathsf{WM}}\varphi^{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.5 -2.5 M -2.5 -2.46 L -2.5 2.46 C -2.5 2.48 -2.48 2.5 -2.46 2.5 L 2.46 2.5 C 2.48 2.5 2.5 2.48 2.5 2.46 L 2.5 -2.46 C 2.5 -2.48 2.48 -2.5 2.46 -2.5 L -2.46 -2.5 C -2.48 -2.5 -2.5 -2.48 -2.5 -2.46 Z M 2.5 2.5}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}

and for
\Gamma^{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.5 -2.5 M -2.5 -2.46 L -2.5 2.46 C -2.5 2.48 -2.48 2.5 -2.46 2.5 L 2.46 2.5 C 2.48 2.5 2.5 2.48 2.5 2.46 L 2.5 -2.46 C 2.5 -2.48 2.48 -2.5 2.46 -2.5 L -2.46 -2.5 C -2.48 -2.5 -2.5 -2.48 -2.5 -2.46 Z M 2.5 2.5}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}\vdash_{\mathsf{IM}}\varphi^{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.5 -2.5 M -2.5 -2.46 L -2.5 2.46 C -2.5 2.48 -2.48 2.5 -2.46 2.5 L 2.46 2.5 C 2.48 2.5 2.5 2.48 2.5 2.46 L 2.5 -2.46 C 2.5 -2.48 2.48 -2.5 2.46 -2.5 L -2.46 -2.5 C -2.48 -2.5 -2.5 -2.48 -2.5 -2.46 Z M 2.5 2.5}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}

by simply replacing the use of
(\mathsf{Mon_{\triangledown}})
by
(\mathsf{Mon_{\hskip 0.62221pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.5 -2.5 M -2.5 -2.46 L -2.5 2.46 C -2.5 2.48 -2.48 2.5 -2.46 2.5 L 2.46 2.5 C 2.48 2.5 2.5 2.48 2.5 2.46 L 2.5 -2.46 C 2.5 -2.48 2.48 -2.5 2.46 -2.5 L -2.46 -2.5 C -2.48 -2.5 -2.5 -2.48 -2.5 -2.46 Z M 2.5 2.5}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.62221pt}})
.
Conversely,
if
\Gamma\not\vdash_{\mathsf{iM}}\varphi
then by Proposition
4.5

there exist a full constructive neighbourhood model
\mathcal{M}=(W,\preccurlyeq,\gamma,V)

falsifying the consecution, so
Theorems
4.2
and
4.8
entail

\Gamma\not\vdash_{\mathsf{WM}}\varphi
and
\Gamma\not\vdash_{\mathsf{IM}}\varphi
.
∎

We complete this section by investigating the relation between
\mathsf{iM}
and
the box-free fragments of
\mathsf{WM}
and
\mathsf{IM}
.
For
\varphi\in\mathbf{L}_{\triangledown}
and
\Gamma\subseteq\mathbf{L}_{\triangledown}
,
define
\varphi^{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -2.95 M -0.03 -2.92 L -2.92 -0.03 C -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 0.01 -2.92 0.03 L -0.03 2.92 C -0.01 2.94 0.01 2.94 0.03 2.92 L 2.92 0.03 C 2.94 0.01 2.94 -0.01 2.92 -0.03 L 0.03 -2.92 C 0.01 -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 -0.03 -2.92 Z M 0 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}
and
\Gamma^{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -2.95 M -0.03 -2.92 L -2.92 -0.03 C -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 0.01 -2.92 0.03 L -0.03 2.92 C -0.01 2.94 0.01 2.94 0.03 2.92 L 2.92 0.03 C 2.94 0.01 2.94 -0.01 2.92 -0.03 L 0.03 -2.92 C 0.01 -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 -0.03 -2.92 Z M 0 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}
as expected.
Then the next example and lemma show that there are consecutions
\Gamma\vdash\varphi

in
\mathbf{L}_{\triangledown}
that are not derivable in
\mathsf{iM}
, but whose
translation is derivable in
\mathsf{IM}
.

4.10 Example
.

Let
W=\{w,v\}
be a two-element set, and order it
by
w\preccurlyeq w\preccurlyeq v\preccurlyeq v
. Define the neighbourhood function

\gamma:W\to\mathcal{PP}(W)
by
\gamma(w)=\emptyset
and
\gamma(v)=\mathcal{P}(W)
,
and let
V
be any valuation.
Then
\mathcal{M}=(W,\preccurlyeq,\gamma,V)
is a constructive neighbourhood model and we have

\mathcal{M},w\not\Vdash\triangledown\top
because
\gamma(w)
contains no neighbourhoods.
Moreover, we have
\mathcal{M},v\Vdash\triangledown\bot
because

\llbracket\bot\rrbracket^{\mathcal{M}}=\emptyset\in\gamma(v)
.
The latter implies
\mathcal{M},w\not\Vdash\neg\triangledown\bot
and

\mathcal{M},v\not\Vdash\neg\triangledown\bot
, and hence we trivially find

\mathcal{M},w\Vdash\neg\triangledown\bot\to\triangledown\top
.
Ultimately, this gives

\mathcal{M},w\not\Vdash(\neg\triangledown\bot\to\triangledown\top)\to\triangledown\top.

It now follows from Theorem
4.2
that

\not\vdash_{\mathsf{iM}}(\neg\triangledown\bot\to\triangledown\top)\to\triangledown\top
.
On the other hand, it is not hard to see that
\mathsf{IM}
proves

(\neg\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to6.52pt{\vbox to6.52pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\;\lower-3.25974pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -4.21 M -0.04 -4.17 L -4.17 -0.04 C -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 0.02 -4.17 0.04 L -0.04 4.17 C -0.02 4.19 0.02 4.19 0.04 4.17 L 4.17 0.04 C 4.19 0.02 4.19 -0.02 4.17 -0.04 L 0.04 -4.17 C 0.02 -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 -0.04 -4.17 Z M 0 4.21}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\bot\to\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to6.52pt{\vbox to6.52pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\;\lower-3.25974pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -4.21 M -0.04 -4.17 L -4.17 -0.04 C -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 0.02 -4.17 0.04 L -0.04 4.17 C -0.02 4.19 0.02 4.19 0.04 4.17 L 4.17 0.04 C 4.19 0.02 4.19 -0.02 4.17 -0.04 L 0.04 -4.17 C 0.02 -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 -0.04 -4.17 Z M 0 4.21}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\top)\to\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to6.52pt{\vbox to6.52pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\;\lower-3.25974pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -4.21 M -0.04 -4.17 L -4.17 -0.04 C -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 0.02 -4.17 0.04 L -0.04 4.17 C -0.02 4.19 0.02 4.19 0.04 4.17 L 4.17 0.04 C 4.19 0.02 4.19 -0.02 4.17 -0.04 L 0.04 -4.17 C 0.02 -4.19 -0.02 -4.19 -0.04 -4.17 Z M 0 4.21}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.86108pt\top
.
So we obtain the following theorem.

4.11 Theorem
.

Let
\Gamma\cup\{\varphi\}\subseteq\mathbf{L}_{\triangledown}
. Then

\displaystyle\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{iM}}\varphi

iff

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -2.95 M -0.03 -2.92 L -2.92 -0.03 C -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 0.01 -2.92 0.03 L -0.03 2.92 C -0.01 2.94 0.01 2.94 0.03 2.92 L 2.92 0.03 C 2.94 0.01 2.94 -0.01 2.92 -0.03 L 0.03 -2.92 C 0.01 -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 -0.03 -2.92 Z M 0 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}\vdash_{\mathsf{WM}}\varphi^{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt},

\displaystyle\Gamma\vdash_{\mathsf{iM}}\varphi

implies

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}\vdash_{\mathsf{IM}}\varphi^{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt},

and the implication is strict.

Proof.

Due to the symmetry of the proof system for
\mathsf{WM}
, we have

\Gamma^{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -2.95 M -0.03 -2.92 L -2.92 -0.03 C -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 0.01 -2.92 0.03 L -0.03 2.92 C -0.01 2.94 0.01 2.94 0.03 2.92 L 2.92 0.03 C 2.94 0.01 2.94 -0.01 2.92 -0.03 L 0.03 -2.92 C 0.01 -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 -0.03 -2.92 Z M 0 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}\vdash_{\mathsf{WM}}\varphi^{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

if and only if

.
Together with Theorem
4.9
, this entails the “iff.”
The implication follows from the fact that the proof system for

\mathsf{IM}
extends that for
\mathsf{WM}
, and the strictness is
witnessed by Example
4.10
.
∎

4.2  Translating
\mathsf{IM}
into multimodal
\mathsf{IK}

The way we defined
\mathsf{IM}
via first-order logic is closely aligned to the
procedure used by Simpson to obtain
\mathsf{IK}
via a suitable
first-order logic
[
40
]
.
As a consequence,
\mathsf{IM}
and
\mathsf{IK}
are strongly related:
intuitionistic neighbourhood frames give rise to frames
for a multimodal version
\mathsf{IK}_{2}
of
\mathsf{IK}
, and we can embed
\mathsf{IM}
into
\mathsf{IK}_{2}
.
We prove that this embedding preserves and reflects derivability,
in the sense that an
\mathbf{L}_{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.5 -2.5 M -2.5 -2.46 L -2.5 2.46 C -2.5 2.48 -2.48 2.5 -2.46 2.5 L 2.46 2.5 C 2.48 2.5 2.5 2.48 2.5 2.46 L 2.5 -2.46 C 2.5 -2.48 2.48 -2.5 2.46 -2.5 L -2.46 -2.5 C -2.48 -2.5 -2.5 -2.48 -2.5 -2.46 Z M 2.5 2.5}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt\!\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -2.95 M -0.03 -2.92 L -2.92 -0.03 C -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 0.01 -2.92 0.03 L -0.03 2.92 C -0.01 2.94 0.01 2.94 0.03 2.92 L 2.92 0.03 C 2.94 0.01 2.94 -0.01 2.92 -0.03 L 0.03 -2.92 C 0.01 -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 -0.03 -2.92 Z M 0 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}
-consecution
\Gamma\vdash\varphi
is in
\mathsf{IM}

if and only if its translation is in
\mathsf{IK}_{2}
.
This mirrors the relation between the classical counterparts
\mathsf{M}
and
\mathsf{K}

of
\mathsf{IM}
and
\mathsf{IK}
, investigated in
[
32
, Section 5.2]
and in more
generality in
[
24
,
35
]
.

be the language generated by the grammar

as follows.

4.12 Definition
.

The modal translation
(-)^{t}:\mathbf{L}_{\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -2.5 -2.5 M -2.5 -2.46 L -2.5 2.46 C -2.5 2.48 -2.48 2.5 -2.46 2.5 L 2.46 2.5 C 2.48 2.5 2.5 2.48 2.5 2.46 L 2.5 -2.46 C 2.5 -2.48 2.48 -2.5 2.46 -2.5 L -2.46 -2.5 C -2.48 -2.5 -2.5 -2.48 -2.5 -2.46 Z M 2.5 2.5}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt\!\hskip 0.60275pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -2.95 M -0.03 -2.92 L -2.92 -0.03 C -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 0.01 -2.92 0.03 L -0.03 2.92 C -0.01 2.94 0.01 2.94 0.03 2.92 L 2.92 0.03 C 2.94 0.01 2.94 -0.01 2.92 -0.03 L 0.03 -2.92 C 0.01 -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 -0.03 -2.92 Z M 0 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.60275pt}\to\mathbf{L}_{2}
is defined recursively by

\displaystyle p^{t}

\displaystyle=p

(for
p\in\Prop\cup\{\bot\}
)

\displaystyle(\varphi\star\psi)^{t}

\displaystyle=\varphi^{t}\star\psi^{t}

(for

)

The idea behind this translation is best understood semantically.
Roughly speaking, the two pairs of modalities in
\mathsf{IK}_{2}
are interpreted
using relations
R_{N}
and
R_{\ni}
, and these mimic the interpretations
of the predicates
\mathsf{N}
and
\mathsf{E}
from the logic
\mathsf{IFOM}
.
We make this intuition precise in Definition
4.16
and the
subsequent lemma.
First, we show that if
\Gamma\vdash\varphi
is derivable in
\mathsf{IM}
,
then its translation
\Gamma^{t}\vdash\varphi^{t}
,
where
\Gamma^{t}=\{\psi^{t}\mid\psi\in\Gamma\}
,
is derivable in
\mathsf{IK}_{2}
.

Suppose

.
Then
\vdash\varphi\to(\psi\to\bot)
.
Necessitation gives
\Gamma\vdash\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M -3.57 -3.57 M -3.57 -3.51 L -3.57 3.51 C -3.57 3.55 -3.55 3.57 -3.51 3.57 L 3.51 3.57 C 3.55 3.57 3.57 3.55 3.57 3.51 L 3.57 -3.51 C 3.57 -3.55 3.55 -3.57 3.51 -3.57 L -3.51 -3.57 C -3.55 -3.57 -3.57 -3.55 -3.57 -3.51 Z M 3.57 3.57}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}

,
and using the axiom
(\mathsf{K_{\hskip 0.62221pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

}

and modus ponens we find

, modus ponens and intuitionistic propositional reasoning
yields

.
The axiom
(\mathsf{N_{\hskip 0.62221pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

and propositional reasoning then yields

\Gamma\vdash\hskip 0.86108pt\text{\hbox to5.6pt{\vbox to5.6pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.7986pt\lower-2.7986pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

}

so that currying gives the required conclusion of
(\mathsf{str}_{j})
.

For the monotonicity rules, combine necessitation, modus ponens,
(
\mathsf{K_{\hskip 0.62221pt\text{\hbox to3.92pt{\vbox to3.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\thinspace\lower-1.95903pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

) and (
\mathsf{K_{\hskip 0.62221pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

.
∎

4.14 Lemma
.

The axioms
(\mathsf{neg_{a}}^{t})
and
(\mathsf{I_{\hskip 0.62221pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{{}{}{{}}}{{}{}{{}}}{}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 -2.95 M -0.03 -2.92 L -2.92 -0.03 C -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 0.01 -2.92 0.03 L -0.03 2.92 C -0.01 2.94 0.01 2.94 0.03 2.92 L 2.92 0.03 C 2.94 0.01 2.94 -0.01 2.92 -0.03 L 0.03 -2.92 C 0.01 -2.94 -0.01 -2.94 -0.03 -2.92 Z M 0 2.95}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\kern 0.62221pt}}^{t})
are derivable in
\mathsf{IK}_{2}
.

Proof.

The former can be proven using
(\mathsf{str}_{\ni})
and
(\mathsf{str}_{N})
, which we have in
\mathsf{IK}_{2}
by
Lemma
4.13
.

,
which is the translation of
(\mathsf{I_{\hskip 0.62221pt\text{\hbox to4.56pt{\vbox to4.56pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 2.28183pt\lower-2.28183pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}

}
